```text
102:   <script>
103:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
104:       const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
105:       const orderId = params.get('orderId') || 'STR-882914';
106: 
107:       document.getElementById('order-confirm-id').textContent = `#${orderId.replace('#', '')}`;
108:       document.getElementById('track-order-btn').href = `track-order.html?tracking=${orderId}`;
109: 
110:       // Look up order in localStorage or STRIDE_DATA
111:       let order = null;
112:       try {
113:         const storedOrders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('stride_orders') || '[]');
114:         order = storedOrders.find(o => o.orderId === orderId);
115:       } catch(e) {}
116: 
117:       if (!order && typeof STRIDE_DATA !== 'undefined') {
118:         order = STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders.find(o => o.orderId === orderId) || STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders[0];
119:       }
120: 
121:       function renderOrderReceipt() {
122:         if (!order) return;
123:         document.getElementById('confirmed-total').textContent = STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(order.total);
124:         const itemsList = document.getElementById('confirmed-items-list');
125: 
126:         itemsList.innerHTML = order.items.map(item => `
127:           <div style="display: flex; gap: 1.25rem; align-items: center;">
128:             <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" style="width: 70px; height: 60px; object-fit: contain; background: #171A22; border-radius: 8px; padding: 4px;">
129:             <div style="flex-grow: 1;">
130:               <h5 style="font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h5>
131:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; margin-top: 2px;">
132:                 ${item.size} • ${item.condition || 'Deadstock'} • Qty: ${item.qty || 1}
133:               </div>
134:             </div>
135:             <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.1rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF;">
136:               ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * (item.qty || 1))}
137:             </div>
138:           </div>
139:         `).join('');
140:       }
141: 
142:       renderOrderReceipt();
143:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderOrderReceipt);
144:     });
145:   </script>
146: </body>
147: </html>
```

```text
176: 
177:   <script>
178:     function renderOrders() {
179:       const container = document.getElementById('orders-list-container');
180:       
181:       // Merge localStorage orders and demoOrders
182:       let orders = [];
183:       try {
184:         const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('stride_orders') || '[]');
185:         orders = [...stored];
186:       } catch(e) {}
187: 
188:       if (typeof STRIDE_DATA !== 'undefined' && STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders) {
189:         STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders.forEach(demo => {
190:           if (!orders.some(o => o.orderId === demo.orderId)) {
191:             orders.push(demo);
192:           }
193:         });
194:       }
195: 
196:       if (orders.length === 0) {
197:         container.innerHTML = `
198:           <div style="text-align: center; padding: 4rem 2rem; background: #171A22; border-radius: 16px; border: 1px solid #292D38;">
199:             <h3>No Prior Orders Found</h3>
200:             <p style="color: #A0A4AE; margin-top: 0.5rem;">When you secure grails on STRIDE, your authentication status and tracking appear here.</p>
201:           </div>
202:         `;
203:         return;
204:       }
205: 
206:       container.innerHTML = orders.map(order => {
207:         let badgeColor = '#FF5A1F';
208:         let badgeClass = 'badge-grail';
209:         if (order.status.toLowerCase().includes('delivered')) {
210:           badgeColor = '#34D399';
211:           badgeClass = 'badge-deadstock';
212:         } else if (order.status.toLowerCase().includes('authenticated')) {
213:           badgeColor = '#00E5FF';
214:           badgeClass = 'badge-vnds';
215:         }
216: 
217:         return `
218:           <div class="glass-card" style="padding: 2rem;">
219:             <!-- Order Header -->
220:             <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid #292D38; padding-bottom: 1.25rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1rem;">
221:               <div style="display: flex; gap: 1.5rem; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
222:                 <div>
223:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Order Number</div>
224:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.05rem; color: #FFFFFF;">#${order.orderId.replace('#', '')}</div>
225:                 </div>
226:                 <div>
227:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Order Date</div>
228:                   <div style="font-size: 0.95rem; color: #FFFFFF;">${order.date}</div>
229:                 </div>
230:                 <div>
231:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Total</div>
232:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.15rem; color: #FFFFFF;">${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(order.total)}</div>
233:                 </div>
234:               </div>
235: 
236:               <div>
237:                 <span class="badge ${badgeClass}" style="font-size: 0.8rem; padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;">
238:                   ${order.status.toUpperCase()}
239:                 </span>
240:               </div>
241:             </div>
242: 
243:             <!-- Items in this order -->
244:             <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
245:               ${order.items.map(item => `
246:                 <div style="display: flex; gap: 1.25rem; align-items: center;">
247:                   <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" style="width: 75px; height: 60px; object-fit: contain; background: #11131A; border-radius: 8px; padding: 4px;">
248:                   <div style="flex-grow: 1;">
249:                     <h4 style="font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h4>
250:                     <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; margin-top: 2px;">
251:                       ${item.size} • ${item.condition || 'Deadstock'} • Qty: ${item.qty || 1}
252:                     </div>
253:                   </div>
254:                   <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.1rem; color: #FFFFFF;">
255:                     ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * (item.qty || 1))}
256:                   </div>
257:                 </div>
258:               `).join('')}
259:             </div>
260: 
261:             <!-- Footer Actions -->
262:             <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-top: 1px solid #292D38; padding-top: 1.25rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1rem;">
263:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; color: #A0A4AE;">
264:                 Carrier: <strong style="color: #FFFFFF;">${order.carrier || 'FedEx Express'}</strong> (${order.trackingNumber || 'FX-998241042-US'})
265:               </div>
266:               <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.75rem;">
267:                 <a href="track-order.html?tracking=${order.orderId}" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">
268:                   Track Delivery
269:                 </a>
270:                 <a href="order-confirmation.html?orderId=${order.orderId}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm">
271:                   View Receipt
272:                 </a>
273:               </div>
274:             </div>
275:           </div>
276:         `;
277:       }).join('');
278:     }
279: 
280:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
281:       renderOrders();
282:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderOrders);
283:     });
284:   </script>
285: </body>
286: </html>
```

```text
295:     function setStep(stepNum) {
296:       const steps = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
297:       const fill = document.getElementById('timeline-progress-fill');
298: 
299:       const percentages = { 1: 0, 2: 25, 3: 50, 4: 75, 5: 100 };
300:       fill.style.width = `${percentages[stepNum]}%`;
301: 
302:       steps.forEach(s => {
303:         const el = document.getElementById(`step-${s}`);
304:         el.classList.remove('completed', 'current');
305:         if (s < stepNum) {
306:           el.classList.add('completed');
307:         } else if (s === stepNum) {
308:           el.classList.add('current');
309:         }
310:       });
311: 
312:       const etaEl = document.getElementById('display-eta');
313:       if (stepNum === 5) {
314:         etaEl.textContent = 'Delivered & Signed';
315:         etaEl.style.color = '#34D399';
316:         STRIDE_APP.showToast('Milestone: Order Delivered & Signed!', 'success');
317:       } else if (stepNum === 4) {
318:         etaEl.textContent = 'Today by 6:00 PM';
319:         etaEl.style.color = '#FF5A1F';
320:         STRIDE_APP.showToast('Milestone: Courier Out for Delivery', 'info');
321:       } else {
322:         etaEl.textContent = 'In 2 Business Days';
323:         etaEl.style.color = '#FFFFFF';
324:         STRIDE_APP.showToast(`Updated to Stage ${stepNum}`, 'info');
325:       }
326:     }
327: 
328:     document.getElementById('tracking-search-form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
329:       e.preventDefault();
330:       const code = document.getElementById('tracking-input').value.trim();
331:       document.getElementById('display-tracking-code').textContent = code;
332:       STRIDE_APP.showToast(`Tracking record loaded for ${code}`, 'success');
333:       setStep(4);
334:     });
335: 
336:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
337:       const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
338:       const trackingParam = params.get('tracking');
339:       if (trackingParam) {
340:         document.getElementById('tracking-input').value = trackingParam;
341:         document.getElementById('display-tracking-code').textContent = trackingParam;
342:       }
343:     });
344:   </script>
345: </body>
346: </html>
```

```text
23:

28:           <h5 style="font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.3;">${item.name}</h5>
29:           <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; margin-top: 2px;">

31:           </div>
32:         </div>
33:         <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.1rem; font-weight: 800; color: #FFFFFF;">
34:           ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * (item.qty || 1))}
35:         </div>
36:       </div>
37:     `).join('');
38:   }
39: 
40:   renderOrderReceipt();
41:   window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderOrderReceipt);
42: });
```

```text
37:       badgeClass = 'badge-vnds';

39:

41:       <div class="glass-card" style="padding: 2rem;">
42:         <!-- Order Header -->

44:           <div style="display: flex; gap: 1.5rem; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
45:             <div>
46:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Order Number</div>
47:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.05rem; color: #FFFFFF;">#${order.orderId.replace('#', '')}</div>
48:             </div>
49:             <div>
50:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Order Date</div>
51:               <div style="font-size: 0.95rem; color: #FFFFFF;">${order.date}</div>
52:             </div>
53:             <div>
54:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.72rem; color: #A0A4AE; text-transform: uppercase;">Total</div>
55:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.15rem; color: #FFFFFF;">${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(order.total)}</div>
56:             </div>
57:           </div>
58: 
59:           <div>
60:             <span class="badge ${badgeClass}" style="font-size: 0.8rem; padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;">
61:               ${order.status.toUpperCase()}
62:             </span>
63:           </div>
64:         </div>
65: 
66:         <!-- Items in this order -->
67:         <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
68:           ${order.items.map(item => `

70:               <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" style="width: 75px; height: 60px; object-fit: contain; background: #11131A; border-radius: 8px; padding: 4px;">

73:                 <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: #FF5A1F; margin-top: 2px;">
74:                   ${item.size} • ${item.condition || 'Deadstock'} • Qty: ${item.qty || 1}
75:                 </div>
76:               </div>
77:               <div style="font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.1rem; color: #FFFFFF;">
78:                 ${STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(item.price * (item.qty || 1))}
79:               </div>
80:             </div>
81:           `).join('')}
82:         </div>
83: 
84:         <!-- Footer Actions -->
85:         <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-top: 1px solid #292D38; padding-top: 1.25rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1rem;">
86:           <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; color: #A0A4AE;">

92:             </a>

94:               View Receipt
95:             </a>
96:           </div>
97:         </div>
98:       </div>
99:     `;
100:   }).join('');
101: }
```

### 1. Current Repository Structure (Branch `i263276`)

All tracked files on `i263276` currently sit in the repository root rather than in `pages/`, `css/`, `js/`, or `assets/` subdirectories:

* **HTML Pages (Pages 13, 14, 15 of the Blueprint):**
  * [order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/order-confirmation.html) — Order Confirmation & Receipt (Page 13).
  * [orders.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/orders.html) — Order History & Collector Ledger (Page 14).
  * [track-order.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/track-order.html) — Live Delivery & Shipment Tracker (Page 15).
* **CSS & JavaScript Files:**
  * [maryamzafar.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.css) — Contains the tracking timeline styles (`.tracking-card`, `.tracking-timeline`, `.timeline-progress-bar`, `.timeline-step` states `.completed` / `.current`, `.timeline-label`, `.timeline-time`, and a `@media (max-width: 768px)` responsive stack). It is not currently linked in `track-order.html`.
  * [maryamzafar.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.js) — Standalone copy of the inline `<script>` inside `order-confirmation.html` (reads `?orderId=`, looks up `localStorage.getItem('stride_orders')` or `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders`, and renders `#confirmed-items-list` and `#confirmed-total` via `STRIDE_APP.formatPrice`).
  * [maryamzafar2.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar2.js) and [Maryamzafar3.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/Maryamzafar3.js) — Identical copies of the inline `<script>` inside `orders.html` (`renderOrders()` merging `localStorage` `stride_orders` with `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` into `#orders-list-container`).
* **Other Files:**
  * [README.md](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/README.md) and two prototype review image files (`Prototype Review pages 13,14,15`, `Prototype_ Review Pages 13,14,15`).
* **Missing Shared Directories on `i263276`:**
  * The HTML files reference `../css/stride.css`, `../js/stride-data.js`, `../js/stride-core.js`, `../assets/logo.svg`, `../assets/logo-mark.svg`, and `../index.html`, but `css/`, `js/`, `assets/`, and `pages/` do not currently exist on branch `i263276`.
  * Across other remote branches in git: `origin/i263197` contains `pages/wishlist.html`, `pages/cart.html`, `pages/checkout.html`, `assets/logo.svg`, and `assets/logo-mark.svg`; `origin/i263185` contains `stride.css` (which imports `stride-design-system.css`, `stride-components.css`, and `stride-pages.css`), `shop.html`, `faq (1).html`, and `order-confirmation.html`; `origin/i263172` has `index.html`, `about.html`, `contact.html`; `origin/i263195` has `login.html` and `signup.html`.

---

### 2. Shared Design System & UI Kit

From the STRIDE Prototyping & Wireframe Blueprint and the existing markup/CSS:

* **Color Tokens (Dark Luxury):**
  * Primary Dark: `#08090D`
  * Panel: `#11131A`
  * Card (`--bg-card`): `#171A22`
  * Subtle Border (`--border-subtle`): `#292D38`
  * Neon Orange (`--neon-orange`): `#FF5A1F`
  * Neon Glow: `#FF8C42` (also used in `logo.svg` / `logo-mark.svg` gradient)
  * Secondary Text (`--text-muted`): `#A0A4AE`
  * Pure White: `#FFFFFF`
  * Status Accents in existing pages: `#34D399` (delivered/ETA) and `#00E5FF` (authenticated badge).
* **Typography:**
  * Headings (`--font-display`): `Outfit`
  * Body: `Inter`
  * Technical Data (`--font-mono`): `Space Mono`
* **Shared Page Chrome & Components:**
  * `.announcement-bar` — Top flash banner (included in `orders.html` and `track-order.html`; omitted in `order-confirmation.html`).
  * `.stride-header` — Sticky/top navigation bar with `.brand-logo` (`assets/logo.svg`), `.nav-links`, and `.nav-actions` (`.trigger-search`, wishlist badge `.wishlist-badge-count`, `.trigger-cart` with `.cart-badge-count`, account link `login.html`, `.mobile-menu-toggle`) plus `.mobile-nav` drawer.
  * `.stride-footer` — `.footer-grid` with `.footer-brand`, 4 link columns (`Marketplace`, `Services`, `Company`, `Social`), and `.footer-bottom` (`© 2026 STRIDE Marketplace Inc.`).
  * Global overlays: `.search-modal-backdrop` and `.cart-drawer`.
  * Shared utility/component classes used: `.container`, `.section`, `.section-tag`, `.section-title-wrap`, `.section-title`, `.section-subtitle`, `.glass-card`, `.btn`, `.btn-primary`, `.btn-secondary`, `.btn-outline`, `.btn-sm`, `.btn-lg`, `.btn-block`, `.badge`, `.badge-grail`, `.badge-deadstock`, `.badge-vnds`, `.text-neon`, `.form-input`.
* **Shared JS Contracts:**
  * `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` (order objects with `orderId`, `date`, `total`, `status`, `carrier`, `trackingNumber`, `items[]` containing `name`, `image`, `size`, `condition`, `qty`, `price`).
  * `STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(amount)` and `STRIDE_APP.showToast(message, type)`, plus the custom `stride:currencychange` window event.
  * `localStorage` key `stride_orders` for persisted orders.

---

### 3. How Your Assigned Pages Fit Into the Project

Based on the files on `i263276` (Pages **13, 14, and 15** — `order-confirmation.html`, `orders.html`, and `track-order.html`):

1. **Directory & Path Alignment:**
   * All three HTML files are authored for the `pages/` folder (`pages/order-confirmation.html`, `pages/orders.html`, `pages/track-order.html`) per page 3 of the blueprint and `origin/i263197`. Moving/organizing them under `pages/` aligns them with `../assets/`, `../css/stride.css`, `../js/stride-data.js`, `../js/stride-core.js`, `../index.html`, and sibling links (`shop.html`, `cart.html`, `checkout.html`, etc.).
2. **End-to-End Post-Checkout Flow:**
   * `checkout.html` (Page 12) → **`order-confirmation.html?orderId=...` (Page 13)** → links to **`track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>` (Page 15)** and `shop.html`.
   * **`orders.html` (Page 14)** lists combined `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) and `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders`, with per-order shortcuts to both **`track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>` (Page 15)** and **`order-confirmation.html?orderId=<orderId>` (Page 13)**.
3. **Asset & Script Integration:**
   * `maryamzafar.css` represents section 7 of the shared stylesheet (`stride-pages.css` imported by `stride.css`) and is required for `track-order.html` to style `.tracking-card` and `.tracking-timeline`.
   * `maryamzafar.js` and `maryamzafar2.js` / `Maryamzafar3.js` currently duplicate inline `<script>` blocks inside `order-confirmation.html` and `orders.html` and should either be consolidated into external scripts or removed to avoid duplicate logic.
4. **Blueprint Alignment Gaps to Keep in Mind:**

Here is a simple implementation plan for your assigned pages (**Pages 13, 14, and 15**: Order Confirmation, Order History, and Live Shipment Tracker) using **HTML, CSS, and vanilla JavaScript only** (no Bootstrap or frameworks, matching the project's custom CSS architecture).

---

### 1. Folder & File Structure Alignment
* **Move pages into `pages/`** to match the blueprint and existing relative paths (`../css/`, `../js/`, `../assets/`, `../index.html`):
  * [order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/order-confirmation.html) → `pages/order-confirmation.html`
  * [orders.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/orders.html) → `pages/orders.html`
  * [track-order.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/track-order.html) → `pages/track-order.html`
* **Clean up duplicate CSS/JS files in root:**
  * Integrate [maryamzafar.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.css) into the shared stylesheet layer (`css/stride-pages.css` imported by `css/stride.css`, or a dedicated stylesheet linked by `track-order.html`) so `.tracking-card` and `.tracking-timeline` styles actually load.
  * Remove redundant duplicates ([maryamzafar.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.js), [maryamzafar2.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar2.js), [Maryamzafar3.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/Maryamzafar3.js)) by consolidating each page's vanilla JS into clean, single-source scripts (e.g., `js/order-confirmation.js`, `js/orders.js`, `js/track-order.js`).
* **Shared Dependencies:**
  * Ensure `assets/` (`logo.svg`, `logo-mark.svg`), `css/stride.css` (design tokens + shared components), `js/stride-data.js` (`STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders`), and `js/stride-core.js` (`STRIDE_APP.formatPrice`, `STRIDE_APP.showToast`, currency/cart/search handlers) are present in the branch so all three pages render and run without missing-file errors.

---

### 2. Shared Theme, Navigation & Components
Follow the Dark Luxury design system (`#08090D` background, `#11131A` panel, `#171A22` card, `#292D38` border, `#FF5A1F` neon orange, `#FF8C42` glow, `#A0A4AE` muted text, `#FFFFFF` text; fonts `Outfit`, `Inter`, `Space Mono`) and standardize the Shared UI Kit across **all three** pages:
* **Top Announcement Bar** (`.announcement-bar`) — add to `order-confirmation.html` to match `orders.html` and `track-order.html`.
* **Global Header** (`.stride-header`) — consistent logo, navigation links (with active state), region/currency selector (`stride:currencychange`), utility actions (Search `Ctrl+K`, Wishlist badge, Cart drawer badge, Account), and `.mobile-nav` drawer.
* **Global Footer** (`.stride-footer`) — consistent 4-column directory (`Marketplace`, `Services`, `Company`, `Social`), flagship hubs (`Tokyo, New York, London, Paris`), and copyright notice.
* **Shared Overlays** — include `.search-modal-backdrop` and `.cart-drawer` on all three pages.

---

### 3. Page-by-Page Implementation Plan

#### Page 13 — `pages/order-confirmation.html` (Order Confirmation & Receipt)
* **Hero & Badge:** Keep the animated checkmark icon, status badge, headline, and `#STR-XXXXXX` monospace order number box (`#order-confirm-id`).
* **Digital Receipt Breakdown:** Extend the receipt card to render from `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) or `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` (via `?orderId=`):
  * Item list (`#confirmed-items-list`) with thumbnail, name, size, condition, qty, and currency-formatted line price.
  * Payment method, delivery carrier & tracking ID, and delivery address (currently missing vs. blueprint p. 16).
  * Total paid (`#confirmed-total`), reactive to `stride:currencychange`.
* **CTAs:** `TRACK LIVE SHIPMENT` (`track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>`), `CONTINUE SHOPPING` (`shop.html`), and `PRINT INVOICE` (`window.print()`).

#### Page 14 — `pages/orders.html` (Order History & Collector Ledger)
* **Order Ledger List (`#orders-list-container`):** Merge `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) with `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` (deduplicated by `orderId`) and display an empty state if none exist.
* **Order Cards (`.glass-card`):**
  * Top header: Monospace `#STR-XXXXXX` ID, order date, currency-reactive total, and dynamic status badge (`AUTHENTICATED`, `IN TRANSIT`, `DELIVERED`).
  * Item strip: Thumbnail, sneaker name, size, condition, qty, and line price.
  * Card footer: Courier + tracking number, `Track Delivery` (`track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>`), and `View Receipt` (`order-confirmation.html?orderId=<orderId>`).

#### Page 15 — `pages/track-order.html` (Live Shipment Tracker)
* **Tracking Lookup (`#tracking-search-form`):** Read `?tracking=` from the URL or search input (`#tracking-input`), match against `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) and `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` by either `orderId` or `trackingNumber`, and update the overview card (`#display-tracking-code`, `#display-eta`, `#display-carrier`, `#display-address`).
* **5-Stage Animated Stepper (`.tracking-timeline`):** Connect `maryamzafar.css` styles and add a subtle pulse animation on `.timeline-step.current` so `setStep(1..5)` transitions smoothly across:
  1. Order Confirmed / Placed
  2. Physical Lab Verified
  3. Tamper-Proof Dispatched
  4. In Transit (Carrier)
  5. Delivered to Customer Door
* **Live Activity Log:** Add the vertical timestamped event log section (blueprint p. 18) that updates dynamically with the current order/stage, while keeping the demonstration simulator buttons functional.

---

### 4. Verification Plan
Once implemented, I will verify:
1. All internal links, relative paths (`../assets/`, `../css/`, `../js/`), and navigation states across the three pages.
2. End-to-end data flow (`?orderId=` → receipt → `orders.html` → `?tracking=` → 5-stage stepper + activity log) using both `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` and `localStorage`.
3. Currency switching (`stride:currencychange`), responsive mobile layout (`<= 768px`), and zero console errors.

Let me know if you'd like me to proceed with implementing this plan (and whether you want me to pull/recreate the missing shared `css/`, `js/`, and `assets/` files on branch `i263276` so the pages run standalone as well).

I have created [pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html) (Page 13 — Order Confirmation & Receipt) using semantic HTML5, the shared STRIDE header/footer/CSS classes (`../css/stride.css`, `../js/stride-data.js`, `../js/stride-core.js`), and vanilla JavaScript, without modifying any other files.

### Important Sections Implemented

1. **Shared Top Bar, Header & Mobile Navigation** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L252-L320))
   * **`.announcement-bar`**: Global vault drop, 8-point lab inspection, and free shipping notice.
   * **`<header class="stride-header">`**: Uses `.brand-logo` (`../assets/logo.svg`), primary `<nav class="nav-links">` (`Home`, `Shop`, `Collections HOT`, `Order History`, `Track Delivery`, `About`), and `.nav-actions` (Search `Ctrl+K`, Wishlist badge, Bag drawer trigger, Account link, and `.mobile-menu-toggle`).
   * **`<aside class="mobile-nav">`**: Slide-out mobile navigation drawer with matching page links.

2. **Celebratory Confirmation Hero** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L326-L343))
   * Centered `.glass-card.confirm-card` inside `<main>` and `<section class="section confirm-section">`.
   * Glowing emerald checkmark badge (`.confirm-check-badge`), `.badge.badge-grail` status tag (`8-POINT LAB INSPECTION QUEUED`), bold uppercase `<h1>` (`ORDER CONFIRMED & QUEUED FOR LAB INSPECTION` with `.text-neon` accent), and confirmation subtitle.

3. **Order Identifier & Date Banner** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L345-L355))
   * `.order-id-banner` (`role="status"`) displaying the `#STR-XXXXXX` order number (`#order-confirm-id`) and `<time id="order-confirm-date">` in `Space Mono` with a neon orange border accent.

4. **Digital Receipt Breakdown (`<article class="receipt-box">`)** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L357-L398))
   * **Purchased Items (`#confirmed-items-list`)**: Displays sneaker thumbnail, title, size/condition/quantity metadata, and line price.
   * **Order Metadata (`<dl class="receipt-details-list">`)**: Structured definition list for **Payment Method** (`#confirmed-payment`), **Delivery Carrier** (`#confirmed-carrier`), and **Delivery Address** (`<address id="confirmed-address">`).
   * **Total Row**: Highlighted **Total Paid** (`#confirmed-total`) in `Outfit` display typography and neon orange.

5. **Post-Purchase Action CTAs** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L400-L408))
   * Primary neon CTA **`TRACK LIVE SHIPMENT`** (`#track-order-btn`, linking to `track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>`), secondary **`CONTINUE SHOPPING`** (`shop.html`), and **`PRINT INVOICE`** (`window.print()`).

6. **Shared Footer, Overlays & Vanilla JS** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html#L415-L558))
   * **`<footer class="stride-footer">`**: Includes `.footer-brand`, flagship hubs (`TOKYO · NEW YORK · LONDON · PARIS`), 4 link columns (`Marketplace`, `Services`, `Company`, `Social`), and copyright notice, plus the shared `.search-modal-backdrop` and `.cart-drawer`.
   * **Vanilla JS**: Reads `?orderId=` from the URL, looks up the order in `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) or `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders`, populates the receipt fields and item list, updates `#track-order-btn`, and re-renders totals on `stride:currencychange`.

- **write_file**: `{"Content":"/* ===================================================================\n   STRIDE — ORDER CONFIRMATION \u0026 DIGITAL RECEIPT STYLESHEET (Page 13)\n   Design System: Dark Luxury with Neon Orange Accents\n   =================================================================== */\n\n@import url(\u0027https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700\u0026family=Outfit:wght@600;700;800;900\u0026family=Space+Mono:wght@400;700\u0026display=swap\u0027);\n\n:root {\n  --bg-primary: #08090D;\n  --bg-panel: #11131A;\n  --bg-card: #171A22;\n  --bg-card-hover: #1E222D;\n  --border-subtle: #292D38;\n  --neon-orange: #FF5A1F;\n  --neon-glow: #FF8C42;\n  --text-secondary: #A0A4AE;\n  --text-primary: #FFFFFF;\n  --accent-emerald: #34D399;\n\n  --font-display: \u0027Outfit\u0027, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \u0027Segoe UI\u0027, sans-serif;\n  --font-body: \u0027Inter\u0027, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \u0027Segoe UI\u0027, sans-serif;\n  --font-mono: \u0027Space Mono\u0027, monospace;\n\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\n  --radius-md: 14px;\n  --radius-lg: 20px;\n  --shadow-card: 0 20px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65);\n  --shadow-neon: 0 0 24px rgba(255, 90, 31, 0.35);\n  --transition-smooth: 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);\n}\n\n/* Base \u0026 Typography */\n*, *::before, *::after {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml {\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\n}\n\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  min-height: 100vh;\n  background-color: var(--bg-primary);\n  background-image:\n    radial-gradient(circle at 80% 10%, rgba(255, 90, 31, 0.1) 0%, transparent 42%),\n    radial-gradient(circle at 15% 85%, rgba(255, 140, 66, 0.06) 0%, transparent 45%);\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n  font-family: var(--font-body);\n  line-height: 1.6;\n  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n}\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5 {\n  font-family: var(--font-display);\n  margin: 0;\n}\n\na {\n  color: inherit;\n  text-decoration: none;\n}\n\nbutton {\n  font-family: inherit;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.container {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 1240px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 0 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.text-neon {\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--neon-orange) 0%, var(--neon-glow) 100%);\n  -webkit-background-clip: text;\n  background-clip: text;\n  color: transparent;\n}\n\n/* Announcement Bar */\n.announcement-bar {\n  background: var(--bg-panel);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);\n  padding: 0.55rem 1rem;\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: center;\n  align-items: center;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 0.75rem 2rem;\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.announcement-bar strong {\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n}\n\n/* Shared Header \u0026 Navigation */\n.stride-header {\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 0;\n  z-index: 100;\n  background: rgba(8, 9, 13, 0.88);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(14px);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);\n}\n\n.nav-inner {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 1.5rem;\n  min-height: 74px;\n}\n\n.brand-logo {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.65rem;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n}\n\n.brand-logo-fallback {\n  display: none;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.brand-logo-text {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  line-height: 1;\n}\n\n.brand-logo-name {\n  font-family: var(--font-display);\n  font-weight: 900;\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\n  letter-spacing: 0.16em;\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n}\n\n.brand-logo-tag {\n  font-family: var(--font-body);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: font-size: 0.9rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-secondary);\n  transition: color var(--transition-smooth);\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.4rem;\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.nav-link:hover,\n.nav-link.active {\n  color: var(--text-primary);\n}\n\n.nav-link:hover {\n  color: var(--neon-glow);\n}\n\n.badge-hot {\n  background: var(--neon-orange);\n  color: display: flex;\n}\n\n.mobile-nav-overlay.open,\n.search-modal-backdrop.open,\n.cart-drawer-backdrop.open {\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* Buttons \u0026 Badges */\n.btn {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 0.55rem;\n  font-family: var(--font-display);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  border-radius: 287:           STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders[0];

```text
292:           return STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(val);

294:         return `$${Number(val || 0).toLocaleString()}`;
```

I have styled your first page (**Order Confirmation & Receipt**) using the STRIDE Dark Luxury design system without modifying any other members' pages or shared files.

### Changes Made
* **Created [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css):**
  * **Design Tokens:** Configured `:root` with Primary Dark (`#08090D`), Panel (`#11131A`), Card (`#171A22`), Border (`#292D38`), Neon Orange (`#FF5A1F`), Neon Glow (`#FF8C42`), Secondary Text (`#A0A4AE`), and White (`#FFFFFF`), plus `Outfit`, `Inter`, and `Space Mono` typography.
  * **Luxury Surface & Glow Effects:** Added subtle radial atmospheric neon lighting on `body`, a top neon hairline gradient and deep shadow on `.glass-card.confirm-card`, a pulsing confirmation badge (`@keyframes confirmPulse`), and a neon-bordered `.order-id-banner` with hover glow.
  * **Receipt & Interactive States:** Styled `.receipt-box`, `.receipt-item-row` (hover lift, border highlight, and sneaker thumbnail hover scale), the `<dl>` metadata grid (`.receipt-details-list`), neon-highlighted `.receipt-total-amount`, and primary/secondary/outline CTA buttons with smooth hover transitions.
  * **Shared Chrome & Responsive Breakpoints:** Included self-contained styles for `.announcement-bar`, `.stride-header`, `.stride-footer`, responsive layouts at `<= 992px` and `<= 768px` (single-column receipt details, full-width mobile CTAs), and a clean `@media print` invoice stylesheet.
* **Updated [pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html):**
  * Linked [order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css) alongside `../css/stride.css` and moved page styling out of inline `<style>` / `style` attributes.
  * Added an inline three-triangle `.brand-logo-fallback` in the header and footer so the STRIDE brand mark renders cleanly even before `../assets/logo.svg` is merged onto branch `i263276`.

### Verification
* Confirmed via `git status -s` that only `pages/` (`order-confirmation.html` and `order-confirmation.css`) was added/modified, and verified all 8 required color hexes are defined and used in [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css).

Let me know when you're ready to refine JavaScript interactivity or build **Page 14 (`pages/orders.html`)** and **Page 15 (`pages/track-order.html`)**.

```text
286:     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
287:       const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
288:       const rawOrderId = params.get('orderId') || 'STR-882914';
289:       const cleanOrderId = rawOrderId.replace(/^#/, '');
290: 
291:       const idEl = document.getElementById('order-confirm-id');
292:       const dateEl = document.getElementById('order-confirm-date');
293:       const trackBtn = document.getElementById('track-order-btn');
294: 
295:       idEl.textContent = `#${cleanOrderId}`;
296:       trackBtn.href = `track-order.html?tracking=${encodeURIComponent(cleanOrderId)}`;
297: 
298:       let order = null;
299:       try {
300:         const storedOrders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('stride_orders') || '[]');
301:         order = storedOrders.find(o => (o.orderId || '').replace(/^#/, '') === cleanOrderId);
302:       } catch (e) {}
303: 
304:       if (!order && typeof STRIDE_DATA !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders)) {
305:         order =
306:           STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders.find(o => (o.orderId || '').replace(/^#/, '') === cleanOrderId) ||
307:           STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders[0];
308:       }
309: 
310:       const formatMoney = (val) => {
311:         if (typeof STRIDE_APP !== 'undefined' && typeof STRIDE_APP.formatPrice === 'function') {
312:           return STRIDE_APP.formatPrice(val);
313:         }
314:         return `$${Number(val || 0).toLocaleString()}`;
315:       };
316: 
317:       function renderOrderReceipt() {
318:         if (!order) return;
319: 
320:         if (order.date) {
321:           dateEl.textContent = order.date;
322:         }
323: 
324:         const paymentEl = document.getElementById('confirmed-payment');
325:         if (paymentEl && order.paymentMethod) {
326:           paymentEl.textContent = order.paymentMethod;
327:         }
328: 
329:         const carrierEl = document.getElementById('confirmed-carrier');
330:         if (carrierEl) {
331:           const carrierName = order.carrier || 'FedEx Express Priority';
332:           const trackingCode = order.trackingNumber || 'FX-998241042-US';
333:           carrierEl.textContent = `${carrierName} (${trackingCode})`;
334:         }
335: 
336:         const addressEl = document.getElementById('confirmed-address');
337:         if (addressEl && order.shippingAddress) {
338:           const addr = order.shippingAddress;
339:           const parts = [addr.name, addr.address, addr.city, addr.state, addr.zip, addr.country].filter(Boolean);
340:           if (parts.length > 0) {
341:             addressEl.textContent = parts.join(', ');
342:           }
343:         }
344: 
345:         document.getElementById('confirmed-total').textContent = formatMoney(order.total);
346:         const itemsList = document.getElementById('confirmed-items-list');
347: 
348:         if (Array.isArray(order.items) && order.items.length > 0) {
349:           itemsList.innerHTML = order.items.map(item => `
350:             <div class="receipt-item-row">
351:               <img src="${item.image}" alt="${item.name}" class="receipt-item-thumb">
352:               <div class="receipt-item-info">
353:                 <h3 class="receipt-item-title">${item.name}</h3>
354:                 <div class="receipt-item-meta">
355:                   ${item.size} • ${item.condition || 'Deadstock'} • Qty: ${item.qty || 1}
356:                 </div>
357:               </div>
358:               <div class="receipt-item-price">
359:                 ${formatMoney(item.price * (item.qty || 1))}
360:               </div>
361:             </div>
362:           `).join('');
363:         }
364:       }
365: 
366:       renderOrderReceipt();
367:       window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', renderOrderReceipt);
368:     });
369:   </script>
370: </body>
371: </html>
```

## Entry 99 — MODEL (PLANNER_RESPONSE)
*Timestamp: 2026-11-28T14:25:37Z*

All interactive functionality for **Page 13** ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html)) is now implemented in vanilla JavaScript inside [pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js), with supporting styles in [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css). No shared files or other members' pages were modified.

---

### How Each Major Feature Works (Viva Guide)

#### 1. Order Resolution, Storage Merging & URL Query Parameters
* **How it works:** On `DOMContentLoaded`, `order-confirmation.js` parses `window.location.search` using `URLSearchParams` to read `?orderId=...` (defaulting to `STR-882914` when no query parameter is supplied).
* **Data merging (`getAllAvailableOrders`):** It safely parses `localStorage.getItem('stride_orders')` inside a `try/catch` block (protecting against corrupted JSON or restricted storage), then merges any shared `STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` and built-in `FALLBACK_ORDERS` (`STR-882914`, `STR-849102`, `STR-741029`) while deduplicating by normalized order ID (`normalizeOrderId`).
* **Normal case:** Renders the order badge status (`#order-status-badge`), monospace order number (`#order-confirm-id`), date (`#order-confirm-date`), itemized sneaker rows (`#confirmed-items-list`), payment method (`#confirmed-payment`), carrier & tracking code (`#confirmed-carrier`), delivery address (`#confirmed-address`), total (`#confirmed-total`), and dynamically sets `#track-order-btn` to `track-order.html?tracking=<orderId>`.

#### 2. Receipt Lookup Form, Validation & Quick Sample Switcher
* **How it works:** `#receipt-lookup-form` intercepts form submission (`e.preventDefault()`) and passes `#receipt-lookup-input` through `loadOrderById()`, while `.demo-chip-btn` buttons allow instant switching between sample orders (`#STR-882914`, `#STR-849102`, `#STR-741029`) and update the URL via `window.history.replaceState`.
* **Validation & Error States:**
  * **Empty input:** Shows inline error feedback (`Please enter an order number such as STR-882914.`) and marks the input `.is-invalid`.
  * **Invalid format:** Checks against `/^(STR-)?[A-Z0-9]{4,12}$/` and rejects malformed IDs with a format error message.
  * **Order not found (`showErrorState`):** Hides `#receipt-breakdown-card`, displays `#receipt-error-state` (`role="alert"`), disables `#copy-order-id-btn` and `#track-order-btn` (`aria-disabled="true"`), and provides a **`Load Latest Demo Receipt`** (`#load-default-order-btn`) recovery button.
  * **Empty items state:** If a matched order object has `items: []`, `.receipt-empty-state` is rendered inside `#confirmed-items-list` with a link to `shop.html`.

#### 3. Copy Order ID & Print Invoice Actions
* **Copy ID (`#copy-order-id-btn`):** Uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` (with a hidden `<textarea>` + `document.execCommand('copy')` fallback for local `file://` testing), toggles the button label to `COPIED` (`.is-copied`), and triggers a toast notification.
* **Print Invoice (`#print-invoice-btn`):** Guarded so it only prints when `currentOrder` is valid (otherwise shows an error toast), triggering `window.print()` with `@media print` rules in [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css) that hide the header, footer, and action buttons.

#### 4. Live Region / Currency Converter (`#region-currency-select`)
* **How it works:** Changing the header dropdown (`USD`, `EUR`, `GBP`, `JPY`) saves `stride_currency` to `localStorage` and dispatches a custom `stride:currencychange` event on `window`.
* **Reactive update:** Listening for `stride:currencychange` re-runs `renderOrderReceipt()` and `renderCartDrawer()`, converting line-item prices and totals dynamically via `formatMoney()` (delegating to `STRIDE_APP.formatPrice` when `stride-core.js` is present, or using `CURRENCY_CONFIG` rates standalone).

#### 5. Working Search Modal, Shopping Bag Drawer & Mobile Navigation
* **Safe Standalone + Integrated Design:** Each control checks `typeof STRIDE_APP !== 'undefined'` (`coreLoaded`) so it does not double-bind events when `stride-core.js` is loaded, while working completely standalone on branch `i263276`:
  * **Search Modal (`.trigger-search` / `Ctrl+K` / `Cmd+K`):** Live-filters sneakers by name, brand, or SKU (`renderSearchResults`), shows popular silhouettes when empty, and renders `.search-empty-state` when no match is found.
  * **Shopping Bag Drawer (`.trigger-cart`):** Syncs `.cart-badge-count` and `.wishlist-badge-count` from `localStorage`, shows `.cart-drawer-empty` when empty, and supports removing items (`[data-remove-index]`) with live subtotal updates.
  * **Mobile Drawer (`.mobile-menu-toggle`) & `Escape` Handling:** Toggles `.open` on `.mobile-nav` and `.mobile-nav-overlay` and closes active overlays on `Escape` or backdrop click.
  * **Footer Social Links:** Updated `IG`, `X`, and `DC` to valid external destinations (`target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`).

---

### Verification
* Checked `git status -s` and `pages/` directory contents: [order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html), [order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css), and [order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js) are present with no edits outside `pages/`.
* Note: `node` is not installed in this environment, so `node --check` did not run; open [pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html) in your browser to test the receipt lookup, currency switcher, copy/print buttons, search modal, and bag drawer live.

## Entry 121 — MODEL (PLANNER_RESPONSE)
*Timestamp: 2026-11-28T14:29:07Z*

I reviewed and updated [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css) with CSS media queries for desktop, tablet, and mobile viewports without modifying any other pages or shared files.

### Responsive & Mobile Fixes Applied

1. **Navigation & Header Usability (`@media (max-width: 1080px)`, `768px`, `560px`)**
   * **Prevented mid-width nav crowding:** Collapsed `.nav-links` into `.mobile-menu-toggle` at `<= 1080px` so the 6-link navigation bar does not collide with `#region-currency-select` and `.nav-actions` on narrower laptops/tablets.
   * **Prevented mobile header overflow (`<= 560px`):** Wrapped `.nav-inner` cleanly onto two tidy rows (centered `.brand-logo` above a full-width, evenly spaced `.nav-actions` row) so all header actions fit comfortably down to `320px` screens.
   * **Compact announcement bar (`<= 768px`):** Reduced vertical padding/font-size and hid secondary announcement items on mobile so the confirmation hero stays visible above the fold.

2. **Touch-Friendly Tap Targets & Comfortable Forms (`<= 768px`, `<= 560px`)**
   * **Minimum 44px touch targets:** Enlarged `.icon-btn`, `#region-currency-select`, `.mobile-nav-close`, `.close-cart`, `.close-search`, `.social-link`, and `.mobile-nav-link` (`min-height: 44px–46px`).
   * **Form & button ergonomics:** Set `.receipt-lookup-input` to `1rem` (`16px`) and `min-height: 46px` (preventing mobile browser auto-zoom on focus), stacked `#receipt-lookup-form` vertically at `<= 560px` with full-width input/submit controls, enlarged `.copy-id-btn` and `.demo-chip-btn` (`min-height: 40px`), and set `.confirm-actions .btn` to full-width `min-height: 48px`.

3. **Responsive Cards, Readability & Overflow Prevention**
   * **Prevented horizontal overflow:** Added `overflow-x: hidden` on `html, body` and `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on `.receipt-detail-val` so long tracking IDs or street addresses wrap cleanly inside cards.
   * **Mobile receipt item layout (`<= 560px`):** Switched `.receipt-item-row` from a squeezed horizontal flex row to a 2-column grid (`72px 1fr`), letting sneaker titles wrap naturally beside the thumbnail with the price aligned cleanly underneath.
   * **Order ID banner & footer stacking:** Stacked `.order-id-banner` and `.footer-grid` into single-column layouts on mobile, centered toast notifications across mobile widths (`.stride-toast-container`), and updated `@media print` to also hide `.receipt-lookup-wrap`, `.copy-id-btn`, `#receipt-error-state`, and toasts.

### Verification
* Verified via `git status -s` (`?? pages/`) that only files inside `pages/` are modified/added, and confirmed the four `@media` breakpoints (`1080px`, `768px`, `560px`, and `print`) in [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css).

Let me know when you're ready to proceed with **Page 14 (`pages/orders.html`)** or **Page 15 (`pages/track-order.html`)**!

```text
313:         trackBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
314:         trackBtn.classList.add('is-disabled');
315:       }
316:       if (copyIdBtn) copyIdBtn.disabled = true;
317:     }
318: 
319:     function renderOrderReceipt(order) {
320:       if (!order) return;
321:       currentOrder = order;
322: 
323:       if (errorBox) errorBox.hidden = true;
324:       if (receiptArticle) receiptArticle.hidden = false;
325:       if (copyIdBtn) copyIdBtn.disabled = false;
326:       if (trackBtn) {
327:         trackBtn.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');
328:         trackBtn.classList.remove('is-disabled');
329:       }
330: 
331:       const cleanId = normalizeOrderId(order.orderId);
332:       idEl.textContent = `#${cleanId}`;
333:       dateEl.textContent = order.date || 'Nov 21, 2026';
334: 
335:       if (statusBadgeEl) {
336:         statusBadgeEl.textContent = (order.status || 'AUTHENTICATION & PACKAGING QUEUED').toUpperCase();
337:       }
338: 
339:       if (trackBtn) {
340:         trackBtn.href = `track-order.html?tracking=${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}`;
341:       }
342: 
343:       if (paymentEl) {
344:         paymentEl.textContent = order.paymentMethod || 'Visa ending in 4242';
345:       }
346: 
347:       if (carrierEl) {
348:         const carrierName = order.carrier || 'FedEx Express Priority';
349:         const trackingCode = order.trackingNumber || 'FX-998241042-US';
350:         carrierEl.textContent = `${carrierName} (${trackingCode})`;
351:       }
352: 
353:       if (addressEl) {
354:         if (order.shippingAddress) {
355:           const addr = order.shippingAddress;
356:           const parts = [addr.name, addr.address, addr.city, addr.state, addr.zip, addr.country].filter(Boolean);
357:           addressEl.textContent = parts.length ? parts.join(', ') : 'Address pending verification';
358:         } else {
359:           addressEl.textContent = 'Address pending verification';
360:         }
361:       }
362: 
363:       const items = Array.isArray(order.items) ? order.items : [];
364:       if (items.length === 0) {
365:         itemsListEl.innerHTML = `
366:           <div class="receipt-empty-state" role="status">
367:             <p class="receipt-empty-title">No Sneaker Line Items Found</p>
368:             <p class="receipt-empty-sub">This order record currently contains no sneaker line items. Contact VIP Concierge if this looks unexpected.</p>
369:             <a href="shop.html" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm">Browse Sneaker Catalogue</a>
370:           </div>
371:         `;
372:       } else {
373:         itemsListEl.innerHTML = items.map(item => {
374:           const qty = Math.max(1, Number(item.qty || 1));
375:           const linePrice = Number(item.price || 0) * qty;
376:           return `
377:             <div class="receipt-item-row">
378:               <img
379:                 src="${escapeHtml(item.image || FALLBACK_SNEAKERS[0].image)}"
380:                 alt="${escapeHtml(item.name || 'Authenticated Sneaker')}"
381:                 class="receipt-item-thumb"
382:               >
383:               <div class="receipt-item-info">
384:                 <h3 class="receipt-item-title">${escapeHtml(item.name || 'STRIDE Authenticated Pair')}</h3>
385:                 <div class="receipt-item-meta">
386:                   ${escapeHtml(item.size || 'US 10')} • ${escapeHtml(item.condition || 'Deadstock')} • Qty: ${qty}
387:                 </div>
388:               </div>
389:               <div class="receipt-item-price">${formatMoney(linePrice)}</div>
390:             </div>
391:           `;
392:         }).join('');
393:       }
394: 
395:       const computedFallbackTotal = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.price || 0) * Math.max(1, Number(item.qty || 1)), 0);
396:       const displayTotal = typeof order.total === 'number' ? order.total : computedFallbackTotal;
397:       totalEl.textContent = formatMoney(displayTotal);
398:     }
399: 
400:     function loadOrderById(rawId, { updateUrl = false, announce = false } = {}) {
401:       const normalized = normalizeOrderId(rawId);
402: 
403:       if (!normalized) {
404:         setLookupFeedback('Please enter an order number such as STR-882914.', 'error');
405:         showErrorState('', 'No order number was entered. Enter an order ID (for example STR-882914) to view its receipt.');
406:         return false;
407:       }
408: 
409:       if (!isValidOrderIdFormat(normalized)) {
410:         setLookupFeedback('Invalid format. Use alphanumeric order IDs like STR-882914.', 'error');
411:         showErrorState(normalized, `Order ID "#${normalized}" is not a valid STRIDE order format. Expected format: STR-882914.`);
412:         return false;
413:       }
414: 
415:       const matched = findOrderById(normalized);
416:       if (!matched) {
417:         setLookupFeedback(`No order found for #${normalized}.`, 'error');
418:         showErrorState(normalized);
419:         return false;
420:       }
421: 
422:       setLookupFeedback(`Loaded verified receipt for #${normalizeOrderId(matched.orderId)}.`, 'success');
423:       if (lookupInput) lookupInput.value = normalizeOrderId(matched.orderId);
424:       renderOrderReceipt(matched);
425: 
426:       if (updateUrl) {
427:         const nextUrl = new URL(window.location.href);
428:         nextUrl.searchParams.set('orderId', normalizeOrderId(matched.orderId));
429:         window.history.replaceState({}, '', nextUrl.toString());
430:       }
431: 
432:       if (announce) {
433:         showNotification(`Receipt #${normalizeOrderId(matched.orderId)} loaded`, 'success');
434:       }
435:       return true;
436:     }
437: 
438:     // Initial order resolution from URL query param (?orderId=...)
439:     const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
440:     const hasExplicitParam = params.has('orderId');
441:     const initialRawId = hasExplicitParam ? params.get('orderId') : 'STR-882914';
442:     loadOrderById(initialRawId, { updateUrl: false, announce: false });
443: 
444:     // Receipt Lookup Form submit
445:     if (lookupForm && lookupInput) {
446:       lookupForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
447:         e.preventDefault();
448:         loadOrderById(lookupInput.value, { updateUrl: true, announce: true });
449:       });
450: 
451:       lookupInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
452:         if (!lookupInput.value.trim()) {
453:           setLookupFeedback('', '');
454:         }
455:       });
456:     }
457: 
458:     // Quick sample receipt switcher chips
459:     sampleChips.forEach(chip => {
460:       chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
461:         const demoId = chip.getAttribute('data-demo-order');
462:         loadOrderById(demoId, { updateUrl: true, announce: true });
463:       });
464:     });
465: 
466:     // Error recovery CTA
467:     if (loadDefaultBtn) {
468:       loadDefaultBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
469:         loadOrderById('STR-882914', { updateUrl: true, announce: true });
470:       });
471:     }
472: 
473:     // Copy Order ID with clipboard + fallback
474:     if (copyIdBtn) {
475:       copyIdBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
476:         if (!currentOrder) return;
477:         const textToCopy = `#${normalizeOrderId(currentOrder.orderId)}`;
478:         try {
479:           if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
480:             await navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy);
481:           } else {
482:             const temp = document.createElement('textarea');
483:             temp.value = textToCopy;
484:             temp.setAttribute('readonly', '');
485:             temp.style.position = 'absolute';
486:             temp.style.left = '-9999px';
487:             document.body.appendChild(temp);
488:             temp.select();
489:             document.execCommand('copy');
490:             temp.remove();
491:           }
492:           const originalLabel = copyIdBtn.textContent;
493:           copyIdBtn.textContent = 'COPIED';
494:           copyIdBtn.classList.add('is-copied');
495:           showNotification(`Copied ${textToCopy} to clipboard`, 'success');
496:           setTimeout(() => {
497:             copyIdBtn.textContent = originalLabel;
498:             copyIdBtn.classList.remove('is-copied');
499:           }, 1800);
500:         } catch {
501:           showNotification('Unable to copy automatically. Please copy the ID manually.', 'error');
502:         }
503:       });
504:     }
505: 
506:     // Print Invoice action
507:     if (printBtn) {
508:       printBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
509:         if (!currentOrder) {
510:           showNotification('Load a valid order receipt before printing.', 'error');
511:           return;
512:         }
513:         window.print();
514:       });
515:     }
516: 
517:     // Prevent navigating to broken link when disabled
518:     if (trackBtn) {
519:       trackBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
520:         if (trackBtn.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true') {
521:           e.preventDefault();
522:           showNotification('No active order loaded to track.', 'error');
523:         }
524:       });
525:     }
526: 
527:     // Region / Currency Selector
528:     if (currencySelect) {
529:       currencySelect.value = getSelectedCurrency();
530:       currencySelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
531:         const nextCurrency = currencySelect.value;
532:         try {
533:           localStorage.setItem('stride_currency', nextCurrency);
534:         } catch {}
535:         window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('stride:currencychange', { detail: { currency: nextCurrency } }));
536:         showNotification(`Currency switched to ${nextCurrency}`, 'info');
537:       });
538:     }
539: 
540:     window.addEventListener('stride:currencychange', () => {
541:       if (currentOrder) renderOrderReceipt(currentOrder);
542:       renderCartDrawer();
543:     });
544: 
545:     // -----------------------------------------------------------------
546:     // Shared Header Controls (Fallback when stride-core.js is absent)
547:     // -----------------------------------------------------------------
548:     const coreLoaded = typeof STRIDE_APP !== 'undefined';
549: 
550:     // Mobile Navigation Drawer
551:     const mobileMenuToggle = document.querySelector('.mobile-menu-toggle');
552:     const mobileNav = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav');
553:     const mobileNavOverlay = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-overlay');
554:     const mobileNavClose = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-close');
555:
```

```text
653:       });
654:       document.querySelectorAll('.wishlist-badge-count').forEach(el => {
655:         el.textContent = String(wishlistCount);
656:         el.style.display = wishlistCount > 0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
657:       });
658:     }
659: 
660:     function renderCartDrawer() {
661:       if (coreLoaded) return;
662:       updateNavBadges();
663:       if (!cartItemsContainer || !cartSubtotalEl) return;
664: 
665:       const cart = safeReadStorage('stride_cart', []);
666:       if (!Array.isArray(cart) || cart.length === 0) {
667:         cartItemsContainer.innerHTML = `
668:           <div class="cart-drawer-empty">
669:             <p>Your shopping bag is currently empty.</p>
670:             <a href="shop.html" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm">Explore Catalogue</a>
671:           </div>
672:         `;
673:         cartSubtotalEl.textContent = formatMoney(0);
674:         return;
675:       }
676: 
677:       let subtotal = 0;
678:       cartItemsContainer.innerHTML = cart.map((item, idx) => {
679:         const qty = Math.max(1, Number(item.qty || 1));
680:         subtotal += Number(item.price || 0) * qty;
681:         return `
682:           <div class="cart-drawer-item">
683:             <img src="${escapeHtml(item.image || FALLBACK_SNEAKERS[0].image)}" alt="${escapeHtml(item.name)}" class="cart-drawer-thumb">
684:             <div class="cart-drawer-item-info">
685:               <div class="cart-drawer-item-name">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</div>
686:               <div class="cart-drawer-item-meta">${escapeHtml(item.size || 'US 10')} • Qty: ${qty}</div>
687:               <div class="cart-drawer-item-price">${formatMoney(Number(item.price || 0) * qty)}</div>
688:             </div>
689:             <button type="button" class="cart-drawer-remove" data-remove-index="${idx}" aria-label="Remove item">✕</button>
690:           </div>
691:         `;
692:       }).join('');
693: 
694:       cartSubtotalEl.textContent = formatMoney(subtotal);
695: 
696:       cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-remove-index]').forEach(btn => {
697:         btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
698:           const index = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-remove-index'));
699:           const nextCart = safeReadStorage('stride_cart', []);
700:           if (Array.isArray(nextCart)) {
701:             nextCart.splice(index, 1);
702:             safeWriteStorage('stride_cart', nextCart);
703:             renderCartDrawer();
704:             showNotification('Removed item from shopping bag', 'info');
705:           }
706:         });
707:       });
708:     }
709: 
710:     function setCartDrawer(open) {
711:       if (!cartDrawer || !cartBackdrop) return;
712:       if (open) renderCartDrawer();
713:       cartDrawer.classList.toggle('open', open);
714:       cartBackdrop.classList.toggle('open', open);
715:       document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
716:     }
717: 
718:     if (!coreLoaded) {
719:       renderCartDrawer();
720:       cartTriggers.forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => setCartDrawer(true)));
721:       closeCartBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => setCartDrawer(false));
722:       cartBackdrop?.addEventListener('click', () => setCartDrawer(false));
723: 
724:       document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
725:         if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
726:           e.preventDefault();
727:           setSearchModal(true);
728:         } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
729:           setSearchModal(false);
730:           setCartDrawer(false);
731:           setMobileNav(false);
732:         }
733:       });
734:     }
735:   });
736: })();
```

## Entry 156 — MODEL (PLANNER_RESPONSE)
*Timestamp: 2026-11-28T14:31:19Z*

I tested [pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html), [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css), and [pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js) against your checklist and fixed the issues found below without modifying other members' pages or shared files.

---

### Problems Found & Fixed

1. **JavaScript Error Guards & Rapid-Click Bug ([pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js))**
   * **Unprotected `localStorage` read:** `getSelectedCurrency()` read `localStorage.getItem('stride_currency')` without a `try/catch`, which could throw in strict privacy modes. Wrapped it in `try/catch` with `'USD'` fallback.
   * **`file://` history API exception:** `window.history.replaceState(...)` inside `loadOrderById()` was unguarded and can throw a `SecurityError` when opening HTML files directly over `file://`. Wrapped it in `try/catch`.
   * **`COPY ID` double-click bug:** Clicking `#copy-order-id-btn` twice within 1.8s captured `'COPIED'` as `originalLabel`, leaving the button stuck on `'COPIED'`. Replaced this with a tracked `copyResetTimer` and an explicit `'COPY ID'` reset.

2. **Broken Image Fallbacks ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html) & [pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js))**
   * **Mobile drawer logo:** The `<img src="../assets/logo.svg">` inside `.mobile-nav-header` lacked the `.brand-logo-fallback` SVG fallback used in the main header and footer. Added the matching `onerror` SVG fallback.
   * **Sneaker thumbnails offline/blocked:** Added `SNEAKER_PLACEHOLDER_SVG` and `attachImageFallbacks()` for `.receipt-item-thumb`, `.search-result-thumb`, and `.cart-drawer-thumb` so failed image loads never render broken image icons.

3. **Navigation Consistency & Semantic `<time>` Sync ([pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html), [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css), [pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js))**
   * **Desktop vs. mobile nav parity:** Added `Sell` (`sell.html`) to desktop `.nav-links` and `Collections` (`shop.html?filter=grail`) to `.mobile-nav-links`, and moved remaining inline styles on `.mobile-nav-footer` and `.cart-drawer` into [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css).
   * **Active sample chip & `<time datetime>`:** Updated `renderOrderReceipt()` and `showErrorState()` to toggle `.is-active` on `.demo-chip-btn` (with matching styles in CSS) and keep `#order-confirm-date`'s `datetime` attribute synced with the active order date.

## Entry 158 — MODEL (PLANNER_RESPONSE)
*Timestamp: 2026-11-28T14:32:48Z*

### 1. Blueprint & Tech Stack Confirmation

* **Technology Stack:** **100% HTML5, CSS3, and vanilla JavaScript** across all assigned pages—no React, Vue, Angular, jQuery, or CSS frameworks are used.
* **Blueprint Alignment Status:**
  * **Page 13 — [pages/order-confirmation.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.html), [pages/order-confirmation.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.css), [pages/order-confirmation.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/pages/order-confirmation.js):** Complete and aligned with Blueprint pages 16 & 19 (folder placement in `pages/`, global announcement bar, header with region/currency selector and utility actions, celebratory hero, `#STR-XXXXXX` order badge, full digital receipt breakdown, action CTAs, 4-column footer with hubs, responsive mobile layout, and full validation/empty/error states).
  * **Page 14 — [orders.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/orders.html)** & **Page 15 — [track-order.html](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/track-order.html) + [maryamzafar.css](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.css):** Present in the repository root using pure HTML/CSS/vanilla JS and matching the core Blueprint p. 17 & p. 18 wireframes (order ledger cards with status badges and tracking/receipt links; 5-stage tracking stepper with search form and stage simulator). *(Note before final team merge: `orders.html` and `track-order.html` are still in the root rather than `pages/`, `maryamzafar.css` is not yet linked inside `track-order.html`, and root duplicate JS files [maryamzafar.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar.js), [maryamzafar2.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/maryamzafar2.js), and [Maryamzafar3.js](file:///c:/Users/Administrator/Documents/SE_D_Group_4_Stride_Sneakers/Maryamzafar3.js) still sit in root.)*

---

### 2. Simple Viva Explanation (HTML, CSS & JavaScript)

#### A. HTML Structure (Semantic & Modular)
Every page follows the same semantic shell so the marketplace feels like one unified product:
1. **Top Bar & Header (`.announcement-bar`, `<header class="stride-header">`):** Holds the STRIDE logo (with an inline SVG fallback if `../assets/logo.svg` isn't loaded), `<nav class="nav-links">`, currency selector (`#region-currency-select`), action buttons (Search, Wishlist, Bag, Account, Mobile Menu), and a slide-out `<aside class="mobile-nav">`.
2. **Main Content (`<main>` + `<section>`):**
   * **Page 13 (`pages/order-confirmation.html`):** Uses a centered `.confirm-card` containing:
     1. **Hero Banner:** Checkmark badge, status pill (`#order-status-badge`), and headline.
     2. **Order ID Banner (`.order-id-banner`):** Monospace `#STR-XXXXXX` order number (`#order-confirm-id`), copy button, and `<time id="order-confirm-date">`.
     3. **Receipt Lookup Bar (`#receipt-lookup-form`):** Input + quick sample order chips + hidden `#receipt-error-state` alert box.
     4. **Digital Receipt (`<article class="receipt-box">`):** Item list (`#confirmed-items-list`), `<dl>` metadata (Payment Method, Carrier, `<address>`), and Total Paid (`#confirmed-total`).
     5. **Action Buttons:** `TRACK LIVE SHIPMENT`, `CONTINUE SHOPPING`, and `PRINT INVOICE`.
   * **Page 14 (`orders.html`):** Renders past and new order cards dynamically inside `#orders-list-container`.
   * **Page 15 (`track-order.html`):** Contains `#tracking-search-form`, `.tracking-card` with a 5-stage `.tracking-timeline` stepper (`#step-1` to `#step-5`), courier/destination details, and simulator buttons (`setStep(1..5)`).
3. **Footer (`<footer class="stride-footer">`) & Overlays:** 4-column link directory, global hubs, copyright, plus the `.search-modal-backdrop` and `.cart-drawer` overlays.

---

#### B. CSS Styling (Dark Luxury Design System)
* **CSS Custom Properties (`:root`):** All brand colors are stored as reusable variables—Primary Dark (`#08090D`), Panel (`#11131A`), Card (`#171A22`), Border (`#292D38`), Neon Orange (`#FF5A1F`), Neon Glow (`#FF8C42`), Secondary Text (`#A0A4AE`), and White (`#FFFFFF`).
* **Typography Hierarchy:** `Outfit` for bold uppercase headings and prices, `Inter` for readable body text, and `Space Mono` for technical data (order IDs, tracking codes, sizes, dates).
* **Visual Polish:** Subtle radial orange glow on the background, glass-style bordered cards with hover elevation, neon gradient primary buttons, `@keyframes confirmPulse` on the checkmark badge, and `@media print` rules that strip navigation/buttons to print a clean receipt.
* **Responsive Media Queries (`order-confirmation.css`):**
  * `<= 1080px`: Switches desktop nav links to the hamburger drawer and makes the footer 2 columns.
  * `<= 768px`: Stacks receipt metadata to 1 column, enlarges touch targets (`min-height: 44px–48px`), and sets form input text to `1rem` (`16px`) to prevent mobile zoom.
  * `<= 560px`: Wraps header controls cleanly onto two rows and uses CSS Grid (`72px 1fr`) on sneaker rows so text never gets cramped.

---

#### C. JavaScript Functionality & Main Logic
All interactivity is written in plain vanilla JavaScript (`DOMContentLoaded`, `URLSearchParams`, `localStorage`, `fetch`-free local data, and standard DOM APIs):

1. **Reading & Merging Orders (`getAllAvailableOrders` & `loadOrderById`):**
   * Reads `?orderId=` from the URL via `URLSearchParams` (defaulting to `STR-882914`).
   * Combines any user orders saved in `localStorage` (`stride_orders`) with built-in demo orders (`STRIDE_DATA.demoOrders` / `FALLBACK_ORDERS`), deduplicating by order ID.
   * **Validation & Error Handling:** Empty inputs or IDs failing `/^(STR-)?[A-Z0-9]{4,12}$/` show inline red validation feedback and reveal `#receipt-error-state` with a one-click recovery button (`#load-default-order-btn`). Missing `items` render an empty-state message with a link to `shop.html`.
2. **Live Currency Conversion (`#region-currency-select` & `stride:currencychange`):**
   * Changing the dropdown saves `stride_currency` to `localStorage` and dispatches a custom `stride:currencychange` event so receipt totals and bag prices recalculate immediately (`USD`, `EUR`, `GBP`, `JPY`).
3. **Copy ID & Print Invoice:**
   * **Copy ID:** Uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` (with a hidden `<textarea>` fallback for `file://`), updates the button to `COPIED`, shows a toast notification, and resets cleanly via a tracked timer.
   * **Print:** Checks that a valid `currentOrder` is loaded, then calls `window.print()`.
4. **Search Modal, Bag Drawer & Mobile Nav:**
   * If the shared `stride-core.js` (`STRIDE_APP`) is loaded, Page 13 defers to it; otherwise, `order-confirmation.js` runs standalone handlers for `Ctrl+K` live sneaker search, cart drawer item rendering/removal from `localStorage`, mobile drawer toggling, and `Escape` key dismissal.
5. **Order History & Live Tracker Logic (`orders.html` & `track-order.html`):**
   * `orders.html` loops through merged orders (`renderOrders()`), maps each order's `status` to a color-coded badge, and builds links passing `?orderId=` to Page 13 and `?tracking=` to Page 15.
   * `track-order.html` reads `?tracking=` (or `#tracking-search-form`) and calls `setStep(1..5)`, which sets `#timeline-progress-fill` width (`0%`, `25%`, `50%`, `75%`, `100%`) and assigns `.completed` / `.current` classes across `#step-1` through `#step-5`.
